Should show: -rw------- 1 user user\n  ```\n\n---\n\n### 🟠 HIGH PRIORITY (Do Within 24 Hours)\n\n- [ ] **Restrict filesystem access**\n  ```yaml\n  # docker-compose.yml\n  services:\n    openclaw:\n      volumes:\n        - ./workspace:/workspace:rw  # Only workspace is writable\n      tmpfs:\n        - /tmp:noexec,nosuid,size=100m\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Block outbound connections to sensitive endpoints**\n  ```yaml\n  # docker-compose.yml\n  services:\n    openclaw:\n      network_mode: "bridge"\n      extra_hosts:\n        - "metadata.google.internal:127.0.0.1"  # Block GCP metadata\n        - "169.254.169.254:127.0.0.1"  # Block AWS/Azure metadata\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Disable unnecessary network ports**\n  ```yaml\n  # Only expose what you need\n  services:\n    openclaw:\n      ports:\n        - "127.0.0.1:8080:8080"  # Bind to localhost only\n      expose:\n        - "8080"\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Add authentication to gateway**\n  ```yaml\n  # In your agent config\n  gateway:\n    auth:\n      enabled: true\n      type: "bearer"\n      token: "${GATEWAY_AUTH_TOKEN}"\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Enable audit logging**\n  ```yaml\n  logging:\n    level: INFO\n    format: json\n    output: /var/log/openclaw/audit.log\n    audit:\n      enabled: true\n      log_commands: true\n      log_file_access: true\n  ```\n\n---\n\n### 🟡 MEDIUM PRIORITY (Do Within 1 Week)\n\n- [ ] **Implement network segmentation**\n  ```yaml\n  # Separate proxy for external access\n    nginx:\n      image: nginx:alpine\n      networks:\n        - openclaw_api\n      ports:\n        - "443:443"\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Set up a reverse proxy with TLS**\n  ```nginx\n  # nginx.conf\n  server {\n      listen 443 ssl http2;\n      server_name openclaw.yourdomain.com;\n  \n      ssl_certificate /etc/nginx/ssl/cert.pem;\n      ssl_certificate_key /etc/nginx/ssl/key.pem;\n      ssl_protocols TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3;\n      ssl_ciphers ECDHE-ECDSA-AES128-GCM-SHA256:ECDHE-RSA-AES128-GCM-SHA256;\n  \n      location / {\n          proxy_pass http://openclaw:8080;\n          proxy_set_header Host $host;\n          proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;\n          proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;\n          proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;\n      }\n  }\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Configure rate limiting**\n  ```yaml\n  # In agent config\n  rate_limit:\n    requests_per_minute: 60\n    tokens_per_minute: 100000\n    burst_allowance: 10\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Set up container health checks**\n  ```yaml\n  # docker-compose.yml\n  services:\n    openclaw:\n      healthcheck:\n        test: ["CMD", "curl", "-f", "http://localhost:8080/health"]\n        interval: 30s\n        timeout: 10s\n        retries: 3\n        start_period: 40s\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Enable automatic security updates**\n  ```bash\n  # Watch for new CVEs\n  docker scout watch openclaw/openclaw:latest\n  ```\n\n---\n\n### 🟢 ONGOING (Weekly/Monthly)\n\n- [ ] **Weekly CVE scan**\n  ```bash\n  # Add to crontab\n  0 2 * * 0 docker scout cves openclaw/openclaw:latest > /var/log/openclaw-cves.log\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Monthly dependency updates**\n  ```bash\n  pip-audit  # Check Python dependencies\n  npm audit   # If using Node.js components\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Rotate API keys quarterly**\n  ```bash\n  # Generate new keys, update secrets, revoke old ones\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Review audit logs**\n  ```bash\n  grep -i "unauthorized\\|failed\\|error" /var/log/openclaw/audit.log\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Backup and test restore**\n  ```bash\n  # Backup\n  docker exec openclaw tar czf - /data > backup-$(date +%Y%m%d).tar.gz\n  \n  # Test restore (on separate instance)\n  docker exec -i openclaw-test tar xzf - -C / < backup-20240115.tar.gz\n  ```\n\n---\n\n## ClawHub Skills Security Checklist\n\n### Before Installing Any Skill\n\n- [ ] **Check the source repository**\n  ```bash\n  # Who owns it? How many stars? Recent commits?\n  gh repo view <skill-repo>\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Review the code manually**\n  ```bash\n  # Look for suspicious patterns\n  grep -r "subprocess\\|exec\\|eval\\|os.system\\|__import__" <skill-dir>/\n  grep -r "requests\\|urllib\\|http\\|socket" <skill-dir>/\n  grep -r "open(\\|write(\\|remove(\\|unlink(" <skill-dir>/\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Check for network calls**\n  ```bash\n  # Should only call known APIs\n  grep -r "https://" <skill-dir>/ | grep -v "api.openai\\|api.anthropic"\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Check for filesystem access**\n  ```bash\n  # Should only access designated directories\n  grep -r "open(" <skill-dir>/ | grep -v "/workspace\\|/tmp"\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **Check for credential access**\n  ```bash\n  # Should not read environment variables or secret files\n  grep -r "os.environ\\|getenv\\|API_KEY\\|SECRET\\|PASSWORD" <skill-dir>/\n  ```\n\n### High-Risk Patterns to Watch For\n\n| Pattern | Risk Level | What It Means |\n|---------|------------|---------------|\n| `subprocess.run(..., shell=True)` | 🔴 CRITICAL | Can execute arbitrary commands |\n| `eval(user_input)` | 🔴 CRITICAL | Remote code execution |\n| `requests.post(external_url, data=...)` | 🔴 HIGH | Data exfiltration |\n| `open("/etc/passwd")` or `/etc/shadow` | 🔴 CRITICAL | Credential theft |\n| `os.environ.get("AWS_SECRET")` | 🔴 HIGH | Accessing secrets |\n| `socket.connect(...)` | 🟠 MEDIUM | Direct network connection |\n| `pickle.loads(user_data)` | 🔴 CRITICAL | Code execution via pickle |\n| `yaml.load(user_input)` without `Loader` | 🔴 HIGH | YAML deserialization attack |\n\n### Safe Skill Installation Process\n\n```bash\n# 1. Download but don\'t install\ngit clone <skill-repo> /tmp/skill-audit\n\n# 2. Run security scan\ncd /tmp/skill-audit\nsemgrep --config auto .\n\n# 3. Review findings\n# 4. If clean, copy to skills directory\ncp -r /tmp/skill-audit /path/to/openclaw/skills/\n\n# 5. Set restrictive permissions\nchmod -R 555 /path/to/openclaw/skills/skill-audit  # Read + execute only\n```\n\n---\n\n## Hardened Docker Compose Template\n\n```yaml\nversion: "3.9"\n\nsecrets:\n  openai_api_key:\n    file: ./secrets/openai_api_key.txt\n  anthropic_api_key:\n    file: ./secrets/anthropic_api_key.txt\n  gateway_auth_token:\n    file: ./secrets/gateway_token.txt\n\nnetworks:\n  openclaw_internal:\n    driver: bridge\n    internal: true\n  openclaw_public:\n    driver: bridge\n\nservices:\n  openclaw:\n    build:\n      context: .\n      dockerfile: Dockerfile.hardened\n    user: "1000:1000"\n    read_only: true\n    tmpfs:\n      - no-new-privileges:true\n      - apparmor:docker-default\n    cap_drop:\n      - ALL\n    cap_add:\n      - NET_BIND_SERVICE\n    restart: unless-stopped\n    logging:\n      driver: json-file\n      options:\n        max-size: "10m"\n        max-file: "3"\n\n  nginx:\n    image: nginx:alpine\n    ports:\n      - "127.0.0.1:443:443"\n    volumes:\n      - ./nginx.conf:/etc/nginx/nginx.conf:ro\n      - ./ssl:/etc/nginx/ssl:ro\n    networks:\n      - openclaw_public\n      - openclaw_internal\n    depends_on:\n      - openclaw\n    restart: unless-stopped\n```\n\n---\n\n## Security Incident Response\n\n### If You Suspect a Breach\n\n1. **Immediately rotate all API keys**\n   ```bash\n   # OpenAI\n   curl -X DELETE "https://api.openai.com/v1/api-keys/{key_id}" \\\n     -H "Authorization: Bearer $OPENAI_ADMIN_KEY"\n   \n   # Generate new key and update secrets\n   ```\n\n2. **Stop the container**\n   ```bash\n   docker-compose down\n   ```\n\n3. **Preserve logs for investigation**\n   ```bash\n   docker logs openclaw > incident-$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S).log\n   cp -r /var/log/openclaw incident-logs/\n   ```\n\n4. **Check for unauthorized access**\n   ```bash\n   # Check API usage\n   curl https://api.openai.com/v1/usage \\\n     -H "Authorization: Bearer $OPENAI_ADMIN_KEY"\n   \n   # Check for unusual patterns\n   grep -i "error\\|unauthorized\\|failed" incident-logs/audit.log\n   ```\n\n5. **Audit installed skills**\n   ```bash\n   ls -la /path/to/openclaw/skills/\n   git -C /path/to/openclaw/skills/ status  # Check for modifications\n   ```\n\n6. **Report and document**\n   - Document timeline of events\n   - Identify attack vector\n   - Update security measures\n   - Consider reporting to OpenClaw maintainers\n\n---\n\n## Quick Security Score\n\nRate your deployment:\n\n| Check | Points | Your Score |\n|-------|--------|------------|\n| Running as non-root | 10 | ___ |\n| API keys in secrets | 10 | ___ |\n| Filesystem restricted | 10 | ___ |\n| Network segmented | 10 | ___ |\n| TLS enabled | 10 | ___ |\n| Authentication enabled | 10 | ___ |\n| Audit logging enabled | 10 | ___ |\n| CVE scanning in CI/CD | 10 | ___ |\n| Skills manually reviewed | 10 | ___ |\n| Budget caps in place | 10 | ___ |\n| **TOTAL** | **100** | ___ |\n\n**Score Interpretation:**\n- 90-100: Well hardened\n- 70-89: Good, room for improvement\n- 50-69: Significant gaps, prioritize fixes\n- Below 50: Critical vulnerabilities, do not deploy to production\n\n---\n\n## Resources\n\n- [Docker Security Documentation](https://docs.docker.com/engine/security/)\n- [OWASP Docker Security Cheat Sheet](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Docker_Security_Cheat_Sheet.html)\n- [CIS Docker Benchmark](https://www.cisecurity.org/benchmark/docker)\n- [Trivy Vulnerability Scanner](https://github.com/aquasecurity/trivy)\n- [Semgrep Security Scanner](https://semgrep.dev/)\n\n---\n\n*Last updated: 2024. Security is an ongoing process. Review this checklist monthly.*\n</create-file>\n</function_calls>'}

You want to automate your work. But every guide you find assumes you\'re a developer.\n\n**This guide is different.**\n\nI\'ll walk you through setting up OpenClaw step-by-step, with copy-paste commands and zero assumptions about your technical background.\n\n---\n\n## Part 1: What You\'ll Need\n\n### Before You Start\n\n**Required:**\n- A computer (Mac, Windows, or Linux)\n- 30-60 minutes\n- $20-50 to spend on API credits initially\n- An OpenAI or Anthropic API key\n\n**Not Required:**\n- Programming knowledge\n- Computer science degree\n- Command line experience (I\'ll teach you)\n\n### Understanding the Costs\n\n| What | Cost | Notes |\n|------|------|-------|\n| OpenClaw software | Free | Open source |\n| API credits | $20-100/month | Depends on usage |\n| Server (optional) | $5-20/month | If running 24/7 |\n\n**⚠️ Warning:** Without proper configuration, agents can burn $200+ in a single night. We\'ll prevent this.\n\n---\n\n## Part 2: Installation (Mac Users)\n\n### Step 1: Open Terminal\n\n1. Press `Cmd + Space`\n2. Type "Terminal"\n3. Press Enter\n\nYou\'ll see a window with a cursor. This is where we\'ll type commands.\n\n### Step 2: Install Homebrew\n\nHomebrew is like an app store for your terminal. Copy and paste this:\n\n```bash\n/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Homebrew/install/HEAD/install.sh)"\n```\n\nPress Enter. Type your computer password when asked (you won\'t see it typed). Press Enter again.\n\n**Wait 5-10 minutes** for installation to complete.\n\n### Step 3: Install Docker\n\nDocker runs OpenClaw in an isolated container (like a mini-computer inside your computer).\n\n```bash\nbrew install --cask docker\n```\n\nAfter installation:\n1. Open Docker from your Applications folder\n2. Click "Accept" on any prompts\n3. Wait for Docker to start (whale icon in menu bar should be steady)\n\n### Step 4: Install Git\n\nGit downloads the OpenClaw code:\n\n```bash\nbrew install git\n```\n\n### Step 5: Download OpenClaw\n\n```bash\ncd ~\ngit clone https://github.com/your-openclaw-repo/openclaw.git\ncd openclaw\n```\n\n**What this does:**\n- `cd ~` - Go to your home folder\n- `git clone` - Download OpenClaw\n- `cd openclaw` - Enter the OpenClaw folder\n\n---\n\n## Part 2: Installation (Windows Users)\n\n### Step 1: Install WSL\n\nWSL lets Windows run Linux tools:\n\n1. Right-click the Start button\n2. Select "Windows Terminal (Admin)" or "PowerShell (Admin)"\n3. Type this command and press Enter:\n\n```powershell\nwsl --install\n```\n\n4. Restart your computer\n5. After restart, a Linux window will open\n6. Create a username and password when prompted\n\n### Step 2: Install Docker Desktop\n\n1. Download Docker Desktop from: https://www.docker.com/products/docker-desktop\n2. Run the installer\n3. Restart your computer\n4. Open Docker Desktop and complete setup\n\n### Step 3: Run Commands in WSL\n\nOpen "Ubuntu" from your Start menu. All commands below should be typed here.\n\n```bash\n# Update package list\nsudo apt update\n\n# Install Git\nsudo apt install git -y\n\n# Download OpenClaw\ncd ~\ngit clone https://github.com/your-openclaw-repo/openclaw.git\ncd openclaw\n```\n\n---\n\n## Part 3: Get Your API Key\n\n### Option A: OpenAI (GPT-4, GPT-3.5)\n\n1. Go to https://platform.openai.com/api-keys\n2. Sign up or log in\n3. Click "Create new secret key"\n4. **Copy the key immediately** - you can\'t see it again\n5. Save it somewhere safe (password manager)\n\n**Pricing:** GPT-4 costs ~$0.03 per 1K tokens, GPT-3.5 costs ~$0.001 per 1K tokens\n\n### Option B: Anthropic (Claude)\n\n1. Go to https://console.anthropic.com/\n2. Sign up or log in\n3. Navigate to API Keys\n4. Create a new key\n5. Copy and save it\n\n**Pricing:** Claude Sonnet costs ~$0.003 per 1K tokens, Claude Opus costs ~$0.015 per 1K tokens\n\n### ⚠️ Important Security Notes\n\n- **Never share your API key** - it\'s like a credit card number\n- **Never commit it to GitHub** - bots will find and use it\n- **Set a spending limit** in your API provider dashboard\n- **Use a separate key for each project** - easier to revoke if compromised\n\n---\n\n## Part 4: Configuration\n\n### Step 1: Create Your Config File\n\nIn your terminal (in the openclaw folder):\n\n```bash\ncp config/example.yaml config/my-agent.yaml\n```\n\n### Step 2: Edit the Config File\n\n**Mac:**\n```bash\nopen -a "TextEdit" config/my-agent.yaml\n```\n\n**Windows/WSL:**\n```bash\nnotepad.exe config/my-agent.yaml\n```\n\n### Step 3: Essential Configuration\n\nReplace the contents with this starter template:\n\n```yaml\n# BASIC SETTINGS\nname: "My First Agent"\n\n# API KEYS - Replace with your actual key\napi:\n  provider: "openai"  # or "anthropic"\n  key: "sk-your-actual-key-here"  # REPLACE THIS\n  model: "gpt-4o-mini"  # Cheaper than GPT-4\n\n# COST CONTROL - Prevent runaway bills\nbudget:\n  daily_limit: 5.00  # $5 max per day\n  monthly_limit: 50.00  # $50 max per month\n  alert_threshold: 0.8  # Alert at 80% of limit\n\n# MEMORY - Prevent forgetting\nmemory:\n  type: "simple"\n  max_history: 20  # Remember last 20 messages\n\n# SAFETY - Prevent disasters\nsafety:\n  require_confirmation: true  # Ask before important actions\n  allowed_directories:\n    - "/workspace"  # Only access this folder\n  blocked_commands:\n    - "rm -rf"\n    - "sudo"\n    - "chmod"\n\n# OUTPUT - Keep it concise\noutput:\n  verbosity: "normal"  # "quiet", "normal", or "verbose"\n```\n\n**⚠️ Change These Values:**\n1. Replace `sk-your-actual-key-here` with your real API key\n2. Adjust budget limits to your comfort level\n3. Choose your model based on the table below\n\n### Model Selection Guide\n\n| Model | Best For | Cost Level |\n|-------|----------|------------|\n| `gpt-4o-mini` | Most tasks, balanced | 💰 Cheap |\n| `gpt-4o` | Complex reasoning | 💰💰 Moderate |\n| `claude-3-5-sonnet` | Coding, analysis | 💰💰 Moderate |\n| `claude-3-opus` | Advanced reasoning | 💰💰💰 Expensive |\n\n**Recommendation:** Start with `gpt-4o-mini` or `claude-3-5-sonnet`.\n\n---\n\n## Part 5: Running Your Agent\n\n### Step 1: Start Docker\n\nMake sure Docker is running (check the whale icon in your menu bar/system tray).\n\n### Step 2: Build and Start\n\n```bash\n# Build the agent (first time only, takes 5-10 minutes)\ndocker-compose build\n\n# Start the agent\ndocker-compose up -d\n\n# Check it\'s running\ndocker-compose ps\n```\n\nYou should see something like:\n```\nNAME         STATUS    PORTS\nopenclaw     running   0.0.0.0:8080->8080/tcp\n```\n\n### Step 3: Access the Web Interface\n\nOpen your browser and go to:\n```\nhttp://localhost:8080\n```\n\nYou should see the OpenClaw dashboard!\n\n### Step 4: Test Your Agent\n\nTry these test prompts to verify everything works:\n\n1. **Simple test:**\n   > "What is 25 multiplied by 47?"\n\n2. **File test:**\n   > "Create a file called test.txt with \'Hello World\' inside"\n\n3. **Web test:**\n   > "Search for the current weather in San Francisco"\n\nIf all three work, your agent is ready!\n\n---\n\n## Part 6: Common Tasks\n\n### How to Stop the Agent\n\n```bash\ndocker-compose down\n```\n\n### How to Restart the Agent\n\n```bash\ndocker-compose restart\n```\n\n### How to Update OpenClaw\n\n```bash\ndocker-compose down\ngit pull origin main\ndocker-compose build\ndocker-compose up -d\n```\n\n### How to Check Logs\n\n```bash\n# See recent logs\ndocker-compose logs --tail=50\n\n# Follow logs in real-time\ndocker-compose logs -f\n```\n\n### How to Check API Usage\n\n**OpenAI:**\n- Go to https://platform.openai.com/usage\n\n**Anthropic:**\n- Go to https://console.anthropic.com/settings/usage\n\n---\n\n## Part 7: Pre-Built Configurations\n\n### Marketing Agent\n\n```yaml\nname: "Marketing Assistant"\n\napi:\n  provider: "anthropic"\n  key: "${ANTHROPIC_API_KEY}"\n  model: "claude-3-5-sonnet"\n\ncapabilities:\n  - web_search\n  - content_writing\n  - social_media\n\ninstructions: |\n  You are a marketing assistant. Help with:\n  - Writing blog posts, tweets, LinkedIn posts\n  - Researching competitors and trends\n  - Creating content calendars\n  \n  Always maintain the brand voice provided by the user.\n\nbudget:\n  daily_limit: 10.00\n\nsafety:\n  require_confirmation: false  # Auto-execute for speed\n```\n\n### Research Agent\n\n```yaml\nname: "Research Assistant"\n\napi:\n  provider: "openai"\n  key: "${OPENAI_API_KEY}"\n  model: "gpt-4o-mini"  # Cheap for research\n\ncapabilities:\n  - web_search\n  - summarization\n  - data_extraction\n\ninstructions: |\n  You are a research assistant. Your job is to:\n  - Search for information on any topic\n  - Summarize findings in bullet points\n  - Cite sources with URLs\n  - Flag conflicting information\n\nbudget:\n  daily_limit: 5.00\n\noutput:\n  verbosity: "quiet"  # Less chatty, more focused\n```\n\n### Personal Assistant\n\n```yaml\nname: "Personal Assistant"\n\napi:\n  provider: "openai"\n  key: "${OPENAI_API_KEY}"\n  model: "gpt-4o-mini"\n\ncapabilities:\n  - email_drafting\n  - calendar\n  - task_management\n  - reminders\n\ninstructions: |\n  You are a personal assistant. Help with:\n  - Drafting emails (professional but friendly tone)\n  - Organizing tasks by priority\n  - Setting reminders\n  - Summarizing information\n  \n  Always ask for clarification if a request is ambiguous.\n\nbudget:\n  daily_limit: 3.00\n\nsafety:\n  require_confirmation: true  # Confirm before sending emails\n```\n\n---\n\n## Part 8: Troubleshooting\n\n### "Docker is not running"\n\n**Solution:**\n1. Open Docker Desktop\n2. Wait for it to start (whale icon should be steady)\n3. Try again\n\n### "Permission denied"\n\n**Solution:**\n```bash\n# Mac/Linux\nsudo chmod -R 755 ~/openclaw\n\n# Or run with sudo\nsudo docker-compose up\n```\n\n### "API key invalid"\n\n**Solution:**\n1. Go to your API provider\'s website\n2. Generate a new key\n3. Update your config file\n4. Restart: `docker-compose restart`\n\n### "Agent is stuck in a loop"\n\n**Solution:**\n1. Stop immediately: `docker-compose down`\n2. Check your budget limits are set\n3. Add loop detection to your config:\n\n```yaml\nexecution:\n  max_iterations: 50  # Stop after 50 steps\n  timeout: 600  # Stop after 10 minutes\n```\n\n### "Agent forgot what we discussed"\n\n**Solution:**\nIncrease memory history:\n\n```yaml\nmemory:\n  type: "simple"\n  max_history: 50  # Increase from default\n```\n\n### "Running out of tokens too fast"\n\n**Solution:**\n1. Switch to a cheaper model\n2. Enable caching:\n\n```yaml\ncache:\n  enabled: true\n  ttl: 3600  # Cache for 1 hour\n```\n\n3. Reduce verbosity:\n\n```yaml\noutput:\n  verbosity: "quiet"\n```\n\n### "Can\'t connect to localhost:8080"\n\n**Solution:**\n1. Check if container is running: `docker-compose ps`\n2. Check port conflicts:\n```bash\n# Mac/Linux\nlsof -i :8080\n\n# Change port if needed\n# In docker-compose.yml, change:\n# ports:\n#   - "8081:8080"  # Use 8081 instead\n```\n\n---\n\n## Part 9: Going Further\n\n### Running 24/7 on a Server\n\nIf you want your agent running continuously:\n\n**Option 1: Cloud VPS ($5-20/month)**\n- DigitalOcean Droplet\n- Linode\n- AWS EC2 (t3.micro)\n- Google Cloud (e2-micro)\n\n**Option 2: Raspberry Pi at Home**\n- Requires ARM-compatible image\n- Limited performance but free\n\n**Setup steps:**\n1. Rent a VPS\n2. SSH into server: `ssh root@your-server-ip`\n3. Install Docker and Git (see Part 2)\n4. Clone OpenClaw\n5. Configure and run\n\n### Adding Skills\n\nSkills extend your agent\'s capabilities:\n\n```bash\n# List available skills\ncd ~/openclaw/skills\nls\n\n# Install a skill (example: web scraping)\ngit clone https://github.com/skill-author/web-scraper-skill.git\n\n# Restart to load\ndocker-compose restart\n```\n\n### Connecting to Other Tools\n\n**Zapier Integration:**\n1. Create a Zapier webhook trigger\n2. Configure OpenClaw to send events to webhook URL\n\n**Slack Integration:**\n```yaml\nintegrations:\n  slack:\n    enabled: true\n    webhook_url: "${SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL}"\n    channel: "#agents"\n```\n\n**Email Integration:**\n```yaml\nintegrations:\n  email:\n    enabled: true\n    smtp_host: "smtp.gmail.com"\n    smtp_port: 587\n    username: "${EMAIL_ADDRESS}"\n    password: "${EMAIL_APP_PASSWORD}"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Part 10: Cost Management Cheat Sheet\n\n### Daily Habits\n- [ ] Check API usage dashboard once per day\n- [ ] Review logs for unexpected patterns\n- [ ] Monitor budget alerts\n\n### Weekly Habits\n- [ ] Review total spend vs. tasks completed\n- [ ] Identify tasks that could use cheaper models\n- [ ] Clean up old conversation histories\n\n### Monthly Habits\n- [ ] Rotate API keys\n- [ ] Update OpenClaw version\n- [ ] Review and update safety rules\n\n### Emergency Response\nIf you see unexpected charges:\n1. **Immediately:** Revoke API key at provider\n2. **Within 5 min:** Stop Docker containers\n3. **Within 30 min:** Check logs for cause\n4. **Next day:** Generate new key, add better limits\n\n---\n\n## Glossary\n\n| Term | Meaning |\n|------|---------|\n| API | Application Programming Interface - how software talks to other software |\n| API Key | Like a password that lets your code access a service |\n| Container | A self-contained package of software |\n| Docker | Software that runs containers |\n| Terminal | Text-based interface for your computer |\n| Token | A chunk of text that AI processes (~4 characters) |\n| YAML | A human-readable format for configuration files |\n| CVE | Common Vulnerability and Exposure - a known security hole |\n\n---\n\n## Getting Help\n\n### Free Resources\n- OpenClaw Discord: https://discord.gg/openclaw\n- GitHub Issues: https://github.com/your-openclaw-repo/openclaw/issues\n- Reddit: r/LocalLLaMA, r/OpenAI\n\n### When Asking for Help\nInclude:\n1. Your operating system (Mac/Windows/Linux)\n2. What you were trying to do\n3. The exact error message\n4. Your config file (with API key removed!)\n\n---\n\n## Checklist: You\'re Ready When...\n\n- [ ] Docker is installed and running\n- [ ] OpenClaw is downloaded and built\n- [ ] API key is obtained and added to config\n- [ ] Budget limits are configured\n- [ ] Agent responds to test prompts\n- [ ] You know how to stop the agent\n- [ ] You know where to check usage\n\n**Congratulations!** You have a working AI agent.\n\n---\n\n*Last updated: 2024. \n        function setPreset(name) {\n            const preset = presets[name];\n            document.getElementById(\'callsPerDay\').value = preset.calls;\n            document.getElementById(\'tokensPerCall\').value = preset.inputTokens;\n            document.getElementById(\'outputTokens\').value = preset.outputTokens;\n calculate();\n        }\n        \n        function updateRangeValue(inputId, displayId) {\n            const value = document.getElementById(inputId).value;\n            document.getElementById(displayId).textContent = parseInt(value).toLocaleString();\n        }\n        \n        function calculate() {\n            const model = document.getElementById(\'model\').value;\n            const callsPerDay = parseInt(document.getElementById(\'callsPerDay\').value);\n            const tokensPerCall = parseInt(document.getElementById(\'tokensPerCall\').value);\n            const outputTokens = parseInt(document.getElementById(\'outputTokens\').value);\n            const daysPerMonth = parseInt(document.getElementById(\'daysPerMonth\').value);\n            \n            const modelPrice = pricing[model];\n            \n            // Calculate tokens\n            const totalInputTokens = callsPerDay * tokensPerCall;\n            const totalOutputTokens = callsPerDay * outputTokens;\n            const totalTokens = totalInputTokens + totalOutputTokens;\n            \n            // Calculate costs\n            const dailyInputCost = (totalInputTokens / 1000000) * modelPrice.input;\n            const dailyOutputCost = (totalOutputTokens / 1000000) * modelPrice.output;\n            const dailyCost = dailyInputCost + dailyOutputCost;\n            const weeklyCost = dailyCost * 7;\n            const monthlyCost = dailyCost * daysPerMonth;\n            \n            // Update display\n            document.getElementById(\'dailyCost\').textContent = \'$\' + dailyCost.toFixed(2);\n            document.getElementById(\'weeklyCost\').textContent = \'$\' + weeklyCost.toFixed(2);\n            document.getElementById(\'monthlyCost\').textContent = \'$\' + monthlyCost.toFixed(2);\n            document.getElementById(\'tokensPerDay\').textContent = totalTokens.toLocaleString();\n            \n            // Cost rating\n            const ratingEl = document.getElementById(\'costRating\');\n            if (dailyCost < 1) {\n                ratingEl.textContent = \'✅ Low\';\n                ratingEl.className = \'result-value success\';\n            } else if (dailyCost < 10) {\n                ratingEl.textContent = \'⚡ Moderate\';\n                ratingEl.className = \'result-value warning\';\n            } else {\n                ratingEl.textContent = \'⚠️ High\';\n                ratingEl.className = \'result-value danger\';\n            }\n            \n            // Color coding for costs\n            const dailyEl = document.getElementById(\'dailyCost\');\n            const monthlyEl = document.getElementById(\'monthlyCost\');\n            \n            if (monthlyCost > 100) {\n                monthlyEl.className = \'result-value danger\';\n            } else if (monthlyCost > 50) {\n                monthlyEl.className = \'result-value warning\';\n            } else {\n                monthlyEl.className = \'result-value success\';\n            }\n            \n            // Update comparison table\n            updateComparison(model, callsPerDay, tokensPerCall, outputTokens, daysPerMonth);\n        }\n        \n        function updateComparison(selectedModel, calls, inputTokens, outputTokens, days) {\n            const tbody = document.getElementById(\'comparisonBody\');\n            tbody.innerHTML = \'\';\n            \n            const selectedPrice = pricing[selectedModel];\n            const selectedDailyCost = ((calls * inputTokens / 1000000) * selectedPrice.input) + \n                                       ((calls * outputTokens / 1000000) * selectedPrice.output);\n            \n            for (const [key, data] of Object.entries(pricing)) {\n                const dailyCost = ((calls * inputTokens / 1000000) * data.input) + \n                                  ((calls * outputTokens / 1000000) * data.output);\n                const monthlyCost = dailyCost * days;\n                const savings = selectedDailyCost - dailyCost;\n                \n                const tr = document.createElement(\'tr\');\n                if (key === selectedModel) {\n                    tr.className = \'highlight\';\n                }\n                \n                tr.innerHTML = `\n                    <td>${data.name}${key === selectedModel ? \' ✓\' : \'\'}</td>\n                    <td>$${dailyCost.toFixed(2)}</td>\n                    <td>$${monthlyCost.toFixed(2)}</td>\n                    <td class="${savings > 0 ? \'savings\' : \'\'}">${savings > 0 ? \'-$\' + (savings * days).toFixed(2) : savings < 0 ? \'+$\' + Math.abs(savings * days).toFixed(2) : \'—\'}</td>\n                `;\n                \n                tbody.appendChild(tr);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // Initial calculation\n        calculate();\n    </script>\n</body>\n</html>\n</create-file>\n</function_calls>'}

Social Media Platform Distrust\\\\n**Trend name:** Reddit API changes and third-party app bans\\\\n**What it is:** Users frustrated with platform decisions that prioritize profit over community.\\\\n**Evidence count:** Multiple threads on Reddit\\\'s API changes\\\\n> \\\\"Users frustrated with Reddit\\\'s push to remove third-party apps and force use of official app\\\\"\\\\n**How long it\\\'s been present:** 1-2 years, with recent acceleration\\\\n**Current status:** Growing platform migration and community fragmentation.\\\\n\\\\n### 3. Dating App Fatigue\\\\n**Trend name:** Online dating platform dissatisfaction\\\\n**What it is:** Users describing online dating as \\\\"enshittified\\\\" with gender imbalances and poor experiences.\\\\n**Evidence count:** ~25 comments on dating struggles\\\\n> \\\\"Online dating platforms are \\\'enshittified\\\' with women receiving vastly more attention than men\\\\"\\\\n**How long it\\\'s been present:** 3-4 years, with recent acceleration\\\\n**Current status:** Growing interest in alternative meeting methods and relationship coaching.\\\\n\\\\n### 4. Passive Income Content Saturation\\\\n**Trend name:** \\\\"Passive income\\\\" scheme skepticism\\\\n**What it is:** Users becoming more aware that most passive income requires significant active work.\\\\n**Evidence count:** ~380 comments on wanting passive income\\\\n> \\\\"SaaS \\\'passive income\\\' requires 20+ hours/week of active work \\\\u2014 support tickets, maintenance, marketing never stop\\\\"\\\\n**How long it\\\'s been present:** 2-3 years, with recent acceleration\\\\n**Current status:** Growing demand for realistic income opportunity assessments.\\\\n\\\\n### 5. Mental Health Treatment Resistance\\\\n**Trend name:** Alternative mental health approaches\\\\n**What it is:** Users describing family members refusing traditional mental health treatment.\\\\n**Evidence count:** ~18 comments on mental health treatment refusal\\\\n> \\\\"Family members describe loved ones fixated on self-diagnoses, refusing all medications including supplements\\\\"\\\\n**How long it\\\'s been present:** 2-3 years, with recent acceleration\\\\n**Current status:** Growing interest in alternative mental health approaches and support systems.\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## Common Questions\\\\n\\\\n### 1. How do I escape a toxic workplace without losing income?\\\\n- **Frequency:** ~35 mentions across threads\\\\n- **Why it goes unanswered:** Users need specific strategies for transitioning without financial risk\\\\n- **Best partial answer seen:** \\\\"Start building passive income streams while still employed, but be realistic about time required\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### 2. How can I afford healthcare when costs keep rising?\\\\n- **Frequency:** ~380 upvotes on healthcare complaints\\\\n- **Why it goes unanswered:** Systemic issues require policy changes, not individual solutions\\\\n- **Best partial answer seen:** \\\\"Look into medical tourism, negotiate cash prices, and consider health sharing ministries\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### 3. How do I deal with family members who financially manipulate me?\\\\n- **Frequency:** ~30+ comments on family financial abuse\\\\n- **Why it goes unanswered:** Emotional manipulation makes boundary-setting extremely difficult\\\\n- **Best partial answer seen:** \\\\"Document all financial interactions and seek legal advice before cutting off support\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### 4. Is passive income really possible, or is it all a scam?\\\\n- **Frequency:** ~380 comments on wanting passive income\\\\n- **Why it goes unanswered:** Most passive income requires significant upfront work and ongoing maintenance\\\\n- **Best partial answer seen:** \\\\"Focus on semi-passive income streams that require 5-10 hours per week of management\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### 5. How can I afford housing in a market where rents keep increasing?\\\\n- **Frequency:** ~15+ comments per thread on rent issues\\\\n- **Why it goes unanswered:** Supply-demand imbalances require systemic solutions\\\\n- **Best partial answer seen:** \\\\"Consider co-living arrangements, rent in less desirable areas, or look at rent-to-own programs\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### 6. How do I start investing when I have no money left after bills?\\\\n- **Frequency:** ~18 comments on financial stress\\\\n- **Why it goes unanswered:** Users need help with both expense reduction and income increase\\\\n- **Best partial answer seen:** \\\\"Start with micro-investing apps and focus on cutting one major expense category first\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### 7. How can I improve my dating life when online dating feels impossible?\\\\n- **Frequency:** ~25 comments on dating struggles\\\\n- **Why it goes unanswered:** Users need help with both approach and self-improvement\\\\n- **Best partial answer seen:** \\\\"Focus on building social circles through hobbies and activities you enjoy\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### 8. How do I handle a partner who controls all the finances?\\\\n- **Frequency:** ~18 comments on financial abuse in relationships\\\\n- **Why it goes unanswered:** Financial control is often tied to emotional abuse and safety concerns\\\\n- **Best partial answer seen:** \\\\"Seek financial counseling together and establish separate accounts for personal spending\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### 9. How can I afford childcare when it costs more than my income?\\\\n- **Frequency:** ~18 comments on childcare costs\\\\n- **Why it goes unanswered:** Childcare costs are structurally high in most developed countries\\\\n- **Best partial answer seen:** \\\\"Look into childcare sharing arrangements with other parents and explore employer benefits\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### 10. How do I know if my workplace is toxic or if I\\\'m just being sensitive?\\\\n- **Frequency:** ~35 comments on toxic work environments\\\\n- **Why it goes unanswered:** Users need objective criteria for identifying toxic behavior\\\\n- **Best partial answer seen:** \\\\"Document specific incidents and compare with industry standards for professional behavior\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## Sentiment Analysis\\\\n\\\\n### Overall Mood\\\\n\\\\nThe community mood is **overwhelmingly stressed and frustrated**, with a strong undercurrent of financial anxiety and relationship breakdown. There\\\'s a pervasive sense that the system is broken and working against them, particularly for parents and those dealing with healthcare costs.
